torch\\\\\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\\\\\nfrom PIL import Image\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nDEVICE = torch.device(\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"cuda:0\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\nCHECKPOINT_PATH = \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-oft-libero-object\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\nUNNORM_KEY = \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"libero_object\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# OFT LIBERO constants\\\\\\\\nACTION_DIM = 7\\\\\\\\nPROPRIO_DIM = 8\\\\\\\\nNUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK = 8\\\\\\\\nNUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS = 8\\\\\\\\nNUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT = 2\\\\\\\\nUSE_PROPRIO = True\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"libero_spatial\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 220,\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"libero_object\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 280,\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"libero_goal\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 300,\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"libero_10\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 520,\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\\\\\n# Action head / projector classes (from openvla-oft, inlined to avoid tf dep)\\\\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass MLPResNetBlock(nn.Module):\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, dim):\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.ffn = nn.Sequential(nn.LayerNorm(dim), nn.Linear(dim, dim), nn.ReLU())\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\\\\\n        return x + self.ffn(x)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass MLPResNet(nn.Module):\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, num_blocks, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim):\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.layer_norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(input_dim)\\\\\\\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        self.relu = nn.ReLU()\\\\\\\\n        self.mlp_resnet_blocks = nn.ModuleList([MLPResNetBlock(dim=hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_blocks)])\\\\\\\\n        self.layer_norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\\\\\n        x = self.relu(self.fc1(self.layer_norm1(x)))\\\\\\\\n        for block in self.mlp_resnet_blocks:\\\\\\\\n            x = block(x)\\\\\\\\n        return self.fc2(self.layer_norm2(x))\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass L1RegressionActionHead(nn.Module):\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, input_dim=4096, hidden_dim=4096, action_dim=7):\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim\\\\\\\\n        self.model = MLPResNet(\\\\\\\\n            num_blocks=2, input_dim=input_dim * ACTION_DIM, hidden_dim=hidden_dim, output_dim=action_dim\\\\\\\\n        )\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def predict_action(self, actions_hidden_states):\\\\\\\\n        batch_size = actions_hidden_states.shape[0]\\\\\\\\n        rearranged = actions_hidden_states.reshape(batch_size, NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, -1)\\\\\\\\n        return self.model(rearranged)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass ProprioProjector(nn.Module):\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, llm_dim, proprio_dim):\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(proprio_dim, llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\\\\\n        self.act_fn1 = nn.GELU()\\\\\\\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(llm_dim, llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def forward(self, proprio):\\\\\\\\n        return self.fc2(self.act_fn1(self.fc1(proprio)))\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\\\\\n# Pruning hook\\\\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass RandomPruningHook:\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, k_ratio=0.5):\\\\\\\\n        self.k_ratio = k_ratio\\\\\\\\n        self.logged = False\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\\\\\\\\n        B, N, D = output.shape\\\\\\\\n        keep = max(1, int(N * self.k_ratio))\\\\\\\\n        indices = torch.randperm(N, device=output.device)[:keep].sort().values\\\\\\\\n        pruned = output[:, indices, :]\\\\\\\\n        if not self.logged:\\\\\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"[Pruning] {N} -> {keep} vision tokens (k={self.k_ratio})\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\\\\\n            self.logged = True\\\\\\\\n        return pruned\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\\\\\n# Args / seed / model loading\\\\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\ndef parse_args():\\\\\\\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\\\\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--k_ratio\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", type=float, required=True)\\\\\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--suite\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"libero_object\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--num_episodes\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", type=int, default=50)\\\\\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--seed\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", type=int, default=7)\\\\\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--output\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", type=str, default=None)\\\\\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--num_steps_wait\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", type=int, default=10)\\\\\\\\n    return p.parse_args()\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\ndef set_seed(seed):\\\\\\\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\\\\\\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\\\\\\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\\\\\\\n    import random\\\\\\\\n    random.seed(seed)\\\\\\\\n    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\\\\\\\\n    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\ndef load_model():\\\\\\\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"[*] Loading OFT model from: {CHECKPOINT_PATH}\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\\\\\n    vla = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\\\\\\\\n        CHECKPOINT_PATH,\\\\\\\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\\\\\\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\\\\\\\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\\\\\\\\n    ).to(DEVICE)\\\\\\\\n    vla.eval()\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    if hasattr(vla, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"vision_backbone\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\") and hasattr(vla.vision_backbone, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"set_num_images_in_input\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"):\\\\\\\\n        vla.vision_backbone.set_num_images_in_input(NUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT)\\\\\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"[*] Set num_images_in_input={NUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT}\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    with open(os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"dataset_statistics.json\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")) as f:\\\\\\\\n        vla.norm_stats = json.load(f)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH, trust_remote_code=True)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    llm_dim = vla.config.text_config.hidden_size\\\\\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"[*] LLM hidden dim: {llm_dim}\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    # Action head\\\\\\\\n    action_head = L1RegressionActionHead(input_dim=llm_dim, hidden_dim=llm_dim, action_dim=ACTION_DIM)\\\\\\\\n    action_head = action_head.to(torch.bfloat16).to(DEVICE)\\\\\\\\n    action_head.eval()\\\\\\\\n    ah_path = os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"action_head--150000_checkpoint.pt\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n    ah_state = torch.load(ah_path, map_location=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", weights_only=False)\\\\\\\\n    if \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"state_dict\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in ah_state:\\\\\\\\n        ah_state = ah_state[\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"state_dict\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"]\\\\\\\\n    action_head.load_state_dict(ah_state)\\\\\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"[*] Action head loaded from: {ah_path}\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    # Proprio projector\\\\\\\\n    proprio_proj = ProprioProjector(llm_dim=llm_dim, proprio_dim=PROPRIO_DIM)\\\\\\\\n    proprio_proj = proprio_proj.to(torch.bfloat16).to(DEVICE)\\\\\\\\n    proprio_proj.eval()\\\\\\\\n    pp_path = os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"proprio_projector--150000_checkpoint.pt\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n    pp_state = torch.load(pp_path, map_location=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", weights_only=False)\\\\\\\\n    if \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"state_dict\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in pp_state:\\\\\\\\n        pp_state = pp_state[\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"state_dict\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"]\\\\\\\\n    proprio_proj.load_state_dict(pp_state)\\\\\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"[*] Proprio projector loaded from: {pp_path}\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"[*] GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\\\\\n    return vla, processor, action_head, proprio_proj\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\\\\\n# Observation processing\\\\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\ndef center_crop_image(image, crop_scale=0.9):\\\\\\\\n    w, h = image.size\\\\\\\\n    new_w = int(w * math.sqrt(crop_scale))\\\\\\\\n    new_h = int(h * math.sqrt(crop_scale))\\\\\\\\n    left = (w - new_w) // 2\\\\\\\\n    top = (h - new_h) // 2\\\\\\\\n    return image.crop((left, top, left + new_w, top + new_h)).resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"stderr\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"return_code\\\\\\\": 0}, \\\\\\\"error\\\\\\\": null, \\\\\\\"metadata\\\\\\\": OFT LIBERO constants\\\\nACTION_DIM = 7\\\\nPROPRIO_DIM = 8\\\\nNUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK = 8\\\\nNUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS = 8\\\\nNUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT = 2\\\\nUSE_PROPRIO = True\\\\n\\\\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\\\\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Action head / projector classes (from openvla-oft, inlined to avoid tf dep)\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\nclass MLPResNetBlock(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(self, dim):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.ffn = nn.Sequential(nn.LayerNorm(dim), nn.Linear(dim, dim), nn.ReLU())\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\n        return x + self.ffn(x)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass MLPResNet(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(self, num_blocks, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.layer_norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(input_dim)\\\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim)\\\\n        self.relu = nn.ReLU()\\\\n        self.mlp_resnet_blocks = nn.ModuleList([MLPResNetBlock(dim=hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_blocks)])\\\\n        self.layer_norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim)\\\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\n        x = self.relu(self.fc1(self.layer_norm1(x)))\\\\n        for block in self.mlp_resnet_blocks:\\\\n            x = block(x)\\\\n        return self.fc2(self.layer_norm2(x))\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass L1RegressionActionHead(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(self, input_dim=4096, hidden_dim=4096, action_dim=7):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim\\\\n        self.model = MLPResNet(\\\\n            num_blocks=2, input_dim=input_dim * ACTION_DIM, hidden_dim=hidden_dim, output_dim=action_dim\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def predict_action(self, actions_hidden_states):\\\\n        batch_size = actions_hidden_states.shape[0]\\\\n        rearranged = actions_hidden_states.reshape(batch_size, NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, -1)\\\\n        return self.model(rearranged)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass ProprioProjector(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(self, llm_dim, proprio_dim):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(proprio_dim, llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\n        self.act_fn1 = nn.GELU()\\\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(llm_dim, llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, proprio):\\\\n        return self.fc2(self.act_fn1(self.fc1(proprio)))\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Pruning hook\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\nclass RandomPruningHook:\\\\n    def __init__(self, k_ratio=0.5):\\\\n        self.k_ratio = k_ratio\\\\n        self.logged = False\\\\n\\\\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\\\\n        B, N, D = output.shape\\\\n        keep = max(1, int(N * self.k_ratio))\\\\n        indices = torch.randperm(N, device=output.device)[:keep].sort().values\\\\n        pruned = output[:, indices, :]\\\\n        if not self.logged:\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\"[Pruning] {N} -> {keep} vision tokens (k={self.k_ratio})\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\n            self.logged = True\\\\n        return
